- ssd/sld unsubscribe wiring — per-account cancel messages (usd+{acct}/uld+{acct}, StreamingOperations.cs:172,187) so unsubscribing one account cannot cancel another's server-side stream; duplicate-cancel suppression keys on full CancelMessage (IbkrWebSocketClient.cs:814-816)
- WIR-5 census non-wiring for ssd/sld judged deliberate-defensible, not a defect: absence of monetary fields is the documented normal state (blank sld rows every 10s; ssd monetaryValue only for pricing/balance keys), so a required-money-field census would be all noise
- Active-probe handler pipeline: IIbkrSessionApi has no TokenRefreshHandler (SessionServiceRegistration.cs:70-100, ServiceCollectionExtensions.cs:36-37), so a 401 during a health check cannot trigger reauth or fight the tickle loop / compete backoff
- Probe transport-failure propagation is deliberate and pinned (HealthStatusCollectorTests.cs:124-144 asserts the original HttpRequestException surfaces), and IbkrHealthCheck wraps any probe exception into Unhealthy (IbkrHealthCheck.cs:45-48) — no scheduler crash through the HealthChecks package
- EvaluateOverallStatus precedence and thresholds (HealthStatusCollector.cs:171-228): Unhealthy checks (auth, token expiry, SES-4/ADR-0004 never-established, staleness) correctly precede Degraded (competing, expiry-warning, streaming, rate-limiter)
- ValidateConnectionAsync flex probe vs rate budget: client-side Flex limiters are wait-not-fail token buckets (1/s burst, 10/min sustained, queue 50 — StreamingAndFlexRegistration.cs:64-82), so the startup validation query only briefly queues, never trips, the consumer's first real ExecuteQueryAsync; server-side Flex throttling remains unpinned but the classification defects there are already reported as ERR-2
- Plain-path provider disposal ordering: IIbkrClient is registered last (ServiceCollectionExtensions.cs:159-163) so MSDI disposes it first — logout precedes WS-client disposal, matching ManagedTenant's documented order; second SessionManager.DisposeAsync from the container is a sequential no-op (SessionManager.cs:441-443)
- SessionHealthState locking and sticky MarkCompeting semantics (SessionHealthState.cs:56-99) match ADR-0004 for the tickle/ssodh/sts paths
- CollectTokenHealth / CollectRateLimiterHealth / CollectStreamingHealth snapshot reads (HealthStatusCollector.cs:125-169) — read-only, no state mutation, null-safe

## Coverage and method notes

- The sweep produced 46 raw findings across nine lenses, deduplicated by a dedicated agent to 41; verification was capped at 40, leaving RST-6 unverified. The gap wave added 12 more skeptic-checked findings, of which 11 survived (PRB-*) and one was refuted (PRB-4.1).
- The completeness critic's four gaps all proved productive: the streaming-facade probe (gap 1) and the ssd/sld money-mapper probe (gap 3) produced three of the twelve high-severity findings (PRB-1.1, PRB-1.2, PRB-3.1 — prefix-only frame routing cross-delivering smd/ssd/sld data between concurrent subscriptions), an interaction between the streaming client and its facade that no single lens owned.
- Zero critical findings, against two in the 2026-07-04 review at c7a07fd: the VCR fix stream (#238–#249) held up under re-review — every VCR fix that agents re-traced was found in place, and the refuted claim STR-1 was struck down precisely because VCR-era code already guards the claimed race.
- PLAUSIBLE verdicts flag exactly the claims whose confirmation depends on live IBKR wire behavior not pinned by `docs/ibkr-web-api-spec.md` or `recordings/` (e.g. AUT-1's server-side OAuth base-string canonicalization, WIR-3's portfolio field sparseness); each names the attended live probe that would settle it.
- Orchestration was throttled to at most 3 concurrent agents throughout (rate-limit safety); total 67 agents over ~2 hours.
- Out of scope: `tools/` (Setup, ApiCapture), `examples/` (read only as evidence), test-suite quality as such, and any live-wire behavior not pinned by the repo's captured recordings.
